## Gate
### unit
PASS. `xcodebuild test -only-testing:PatinaTests -configuration Debug` on clone P (BD8D6AC8-BA6C-484B-B819-6E671FA72D8D) with its own DerivedData at `.build/dd-gate` returned `** TEST SUCCEEDED **`, exit 0: `✔ Test run with 1523 tests in 164 suites passed after 4.733 seconds.` — 1523 executed, 1523 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, 0 known issues, 163 `✔ Suite` lines and zero `✘` lines anywhere in the log. No failing test names to report. The test count matches the 1523 recorded by the Daily Return program, so nothing has rotted since. The one problem is wall clock, not outcome: the run took 610 s (`IDETestOperationsObserverDebug: 610.538 elapsed`) for 4.7 s of assertions, because xcodebuild spawned `simctl diagnose -l -b --timeout=600` at the end and it never answered — `Failure collecting diagnostics from simulator: Timed out after 600.0 seconds while waiting for a response from the invoked process` (finding G-10; observed once, on a clone the walkers had been driving, so it may be device-state-specific). The resulting xcresult is 159 MB at `/Users/kody/Code/patina-merged/artifacts/ios-testflight-polish-2026-09-01/.build/dd-gate/Logs/Test/Test-Patina-2026.09.01_17-57-54--0500.xcresult`. The build inside this run emitted 1604 warning lines, 1330 of them "this is an error in the Swift 6 language mode" (finding G-09). Log: `/Users/kody/Code/patina-merged/artifacts/ios-testflight-polish-2026-09-01/research/G-unit.log`.
### ui
FAIL — and it did not time out; the whole tier finished in 146 s, far inside the 20-minute budget. `** TEST FAILED **`, exit 65, `Executed 11 tests, with 7 failures (0 unexpected) in 113.036 seconds`. All seven failures are in `FirstLaunchUITests` and all have one root cause: `XCTAssertTrue failed - Threshold should appear` (`PatinaUITests/Helpers/FirstLaunchTestHelpers.swift:99`) / `Threshold enter button should appear` (`PatinaUITests/FirstLaunchUITests.swift:121`). Failing tests: `testDriftAction`, `testHappyPath`, `testLaunchPerformance`, `testNotYetPath`, `testThresholdToWalkInvitationTransition`, `testThresholdUI`, `testWalkInvitationUI`. The helper waits on `otherElements["threshold.enterButton"]`, an identifier that appears nowhere in the app source (`grep -rn "threshold.enterButton" apps/mobile/Patina/Patina` → 0 hits), and the suite asserts literal copy from the retired spec `docs/specs/_active/mobile-first-launch.md` ("Every room\ntells a story.", "Shall we walk your space together?"). `git log` on both the suite and its helpers returns exactly one commit — `9a3209bf2 chore: initial monorepo setup for Patina platform` — so this has been red since the repo was created and no gate runs it. The four "passing" tests are unmodified Xcode template stubs: `PatinaUITests.testExample` (launches, asserts nothing), `PatinaUITests.testLaunchPerformance` (`measure { XCUIApplication().launch() }`), and `PatinaUITestsLaunchTests.testLaunch` ×2 (screenshot only). Net: zero passing product assertions (finding G-03). One usable datum fell out: `measured [Duration (AppLaunch), s] average: 2.131, relative standard deviation: 3.698%` — Debug build on simulator, not a Release cold-launch number. Log: `/Users/kody/Code/patina-merged/artifacts/ios-testflight-polish-2026-09-01/research/G-ui.log`.
### release_compile
FAIL — this is the headline. `** BUILD FAILED **`, exit 65, `(3 failures)`, six compiler errors, all the same shape: `AddToRoomSheet.swift:98:26 error: type 'Product' has no member 'previewProducts'`, `AddToRoomSheet.swift:99:28 error: type 'RoomSummary' has no member 'mockAll'`, `DailyStoryCard.swift:132:28 error: type 'DailyStory' has no member 'preview'`, `DailyStoryDetailView.swift:214:72` and `:214:38` (same two types), `ProductDetailView.swift:693:40 error: type 'Product' has no member 'previewProducts'`. Root cause: the fixtures are `#if DEBUG`-gated (`ProductModel.swift:361` literally comments "`previewProducts` is intentionally `#if DEBUG`-gated and only used by SwiftUI `#Preview` blocks") but the four `#Preview` blocks that consume them are not — and `ENABLE_PREVIEWS = NO` in Release does not strip a `#Preview` macro body, it still expands and type-checks. A census over all 435 Swift files found 92 `#Preview` blocks (8 guarded, 84 unguarded) cross-checked against the 18 `#if DEBUG`-declared static members; only these 4 files are real hits, the other candidates pass the string literal `"preview"` as an id. `SWIFT_COMPILATION_MODE = wholemodule` in Release, so all module errors were reported in one pass — the break is bounded to 4 files / 6 call sites (finding G-01, effort S). On Release-specific warnings: **there are none**. The Release log has 222 warning lines / 103 distinct texts, and `comm -13` against the deduped Debug set (`.build/xcodebuild.log`, 389 warnings / 110 distinct) yields an empty release-only set; the Release log is only shorter because the build aborted at the app target's Swift compile. Two build-phase notes appear in both configurations: `note: Run script build phase 'Stamp Git SHA' will be run during every build because the option to run the script phase "Based on dependency analysis" is unchecked` and `warning: Run script build phase 'SwiftLint' will be run during every build because it does not specify any outputs`. Log: `/Users/kody/Code/patina-merged/artifacts/ios-testflight-polish-2026-09-01/research/G-release.log`.
### lint
933 violations — 512 warning, **421 error** — across 144 files; swiftlint 0.63.2 exits 2, so the `lint` tier can never be green. One config warning: "Found a configuration for 'line_length' rule, but it is disabled in 'disabled_rules'." Top 15 rules: `identifier_name` 532 (396 error / 136 warning), `trailing_comma` 67, `redundant_string_enum_value` 59, `disallow_font_custom_in_features` 48 (custom PT-1-1), `nesting` 30, `implicit_optional_initialization` 26, `function_body_length` 25 (4 error), `modifier_order` 21, `file_length` 20 (2 error), `multiple_closures_with_trailing_closure` 18, `type_body_length` 13 (4 error), `vertical_parameter_alignment_on_call` 11, `cyclomatic_complexity` 10 (3 error), `function_parameter_count` 9 (3 error), `comma` 7. Top 15 files: `Core/Network/RoomsAPIClient.swift` 66, `Services/API/ProposalsAPIClient.swift` 55, `Services/Sync/Models/RoomScanV2DTOs.swift` 49, `Core/Network/ProjectsAPIClient.swift` 47, `Core/Network/FulfillmentAPIClient.swift` 38, `Services/API/InvoicesAPIClient.swift` 30, `Core/Network/DecisionsAPIClient.swift` 29, `Core/Network/MessagingAPIClient.swift` 29, `Services/Sync/Models/RoomScanSyncDTOs.swift` 28, `Services/Analytics/HelpAnalytics.swift` 22, `Features/RoomScan/Views/ScanReviewView.swift` 18, `Services/Sync/RoomScanSyncService+AdvancedBundle.swift` 16, `Features/Conversation/Models/ConversationState.swift` 14, `Services/Sharing/ScanSharingService.swift` 14, `Features/Walk/Models/ScanManifest.swift` 13. All 396 `identifier_name` errors are one benign bucket — snake_case DTO properties mirroring Postgres columns (`designer_id`, `created_at`, `stripe_payment_intent_id`) — which is what makes the tier unusable (finding G-11). Good news: the three error-severity design-system custom rules `disallow_foregroundcolor`, `disallow_cornerradius` and `disallow_navigation_bar_hidden` have **zero hits**, so those codemod sweeps genuinely landed. Notable individual hits: 3 raw `print()` in shipping code (`RoomCaptureService+Instrument.swift:116,123`, `UploadDiagnosticsLog.swift:13`), 2 `DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter` (`ScanThresholdView.swift:80`, `QRScannerView.swift:362`), 1 unresolved TODO (`AestheteEngineService.swift:189`), `CompanionOverlay.swift` at 1160 lines. 27 violations sit inside the test targets. JSON: `/Users/kody/Code/patina-merged/artifacts/ios-testflight-polish-2026-09-01/research/G-lint.json`.
### archive
FAIL, with exactly the same six compile errors — `** ARCHIVE FAILED **`, exit 65, `(3 failures)`; `.build/Patina.xcarchive` was never created. I did **not** retry: the brief's retry allowance is for signing/provisioning/capability failures, and this is a compile error that a second identical run cannot change. Consequently steps 5a–5d (embedded entitlements of the archived app, its CFBundleVersion / CFBundleShortVersionString / MinimumOSVersion, PatinaWidget.appex inside PlugIns, PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy in the archive) are unverifiable until G-01 is fixed; nothing was exported or uploaded. Signing readiness did, however, get exercised before the compile failed, and it looks healthy: both certificates exist in the keychain (`Apple Development: Kody Kochaver (BD8AHP9A59)` and `Apple Distribution: Middle West Studio LLC (VP22LXHT7L)`); both team provisioning profiles are present and valid to 2027-08-29 (`c62c6642…` → `VP22LXHT7L.cloud.patina.app`, `7cd87e95…` → `VP22LXHT7L.cloud.patina.app.widget`, TeamName "Middle West Studio LLC"); and the widget target compiled, packaged and **signed successfully** — `Signing Identity: "Apple Development: Kody Kochaver (BD8AHP9A59)"`, `Provisioning Profile: "iOS Team Provisioning Profile: cloud.patina.app.widget"`. The app-target entitlements as processed for the archive were `application-identifier VP22LXHT7L.cloud.patina.app`, `aps-environment = development`, `com.apple.developer.applesignin = (Default)`, `com.apple.developer.associated-domains = (applinks:client.patina.cloud)`, `com.apple.developer.team-identifier VP22LXHT7L`, `com.apple.security.application-groups = (group.cloud.patina.app)`, `get-task-allow = 1` — the last two coming from the development profile the archive action selected (risk G-12, confidence 0.5, since automatic-signing export normally rewrites both). Release build settings are otherwise exactly right for TestFlight: `DEBUG_INFORMATION_FORMAT = dwarf-with-dsym`, `STRIP_INSTALLED_PRODUCT = YES`, `STRIP_STYLE = all`, `VALIDATE_PRODUCT = YES`, `ENABLE_TESTABILITY = NO`, `wholemodule`, `DEAD_CODE_STRIPPING = YES`, `ENABLE_DEBUG_DYLIB = NO`, `CODE_SIGN_STYLE = Automatic`, `DEVELOPMENT_TEAM = VP22LXHT7L`, `MARKETING_VERSION = 1.0`, `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION = 1`. Logs: `/Users/kody/Code/patina-merged/artifacts/ios-testflight-polish-2026-09-01/research/G-archive.log`, settings dump at `research/G-buildsettings-release.txt`.
### built_app_inspection
The steward's Debug simulator build at `/Users/kody/Code/patina-merged/artifacts/ios-testflight-polish-2026-09-01/.build/DerivedData/Build/Products/Debug-iphonesimulator/Patina.app` is 69 MB and clean. Icons are correct and worth calling out: `Info.plist` has `CFBundleIcons.CFBundlePrimaryIcon.CFBundleIconName = AppIcon` with `CFBundleIconFiles = [AppIcon60x60]` plus a `CFBundleIcons~ipad` variant (no top-level `CFBundleIconName`, which is normal for this pipeline), and `assetutil --info Assets.car` confirms the icon **is** compiled from the Icon Composer file `Patina/Resources/AppIcon.icon` — three `AppIcon.iconstack` renditions, the layered groups `AppIcon/Group 1_ Background`, `Group 2_ Middleground`, `Group 3_ Foreground`, the `AppIcon_Assets/{Color-1…7, Gradient-1…4, 01-background, 02-strata 1, PSVG}` sources, and all three 1024×1024 phone fallbacks `UIAppearanceAny` (507 KB), `UIAppearanceDark` (536 KB) and `ISAppearanceTintable` (377 KB); the catalog header reports `Appearances: {ISAppearanceTintable: 10, UIAppearanceAny: 0, UIAppearanceDark: 1, UIAppearanceLight: 4}`. Light, dark and tinted are all present — the iOS 26 icon contract is fully met, and `Assets.car` contains nothing but the app icon. Bundle root ls: `__preview.dylib`, `_CodeSignature/`, `AppIcon60x60@2x.png`, `AppIcon76x76@2x~ipad.png`, `Assets.car`, `Frameworks/PatinaDesignKit.framework`, `Info.plist`, `Patina`, `Patina.debug.dylib`, `PatinaDesignKit_PatinaDesignKit.bundle`, `PkgInfo`, `PLCrashReporter_CrashReporter.bundle`, `PlugIns/PatinaWidget.appex`, `PostHog_PostHog.bundle`, `swift-crypto_Crypto.bundle`. No `.DS_Store`, no `.swiftmodule`/`.xcconfig`/`.md`/`.sh`/`.map` anywhere, and `Patina.debug.dylib` + `__preview.dylib` are Debug-only (`ENABLE_DEBUG_DYLIB = NO` in Release) so they do not ship. Ten largest files (KB): `Patina.debug.dylib` 64536, `Assets.car` 1420, `PatinaDesignKit.framework/PatinaDesignKit` 1296, `PlugIns/PatinaWidget.appex/PatinaWidget.debug.dylib` 428, then eight duplicated TTFs at 320/124 KB — Inter Regular/Medium/SemiBold and Playfair Regular/Medium exist in **both** `PatinaDesignKit_PatinaDesignKit.bundle` and `PlugIns/PatinaWidget.appex/PatinaDesignKit_PatinaDesignKit.bundle` (~1.4 MB shipped twice, finding G-13). `PatinaWidget.appex` is correctly inside `PlugIns/` with `NSExtensionPointIdentifier = com.apple.widgetkit-extension`, bundle id `cloud.patina.app.widget`, `MinimumOSVersion 26.5`, `UIDeviceFamily [1,2]`. The only `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` files in the bundle come from dependencies (swift-crypto, PLCrashReporter, PostHog) — **the app itself ships none**, while the sibling app has one at `apps/mobile/Capture/Capture/PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` (finding G-05). Shipped Info.plist also lacks `ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption` (G-06), carries `UIDeviceFamily [1,2]` with portrait-only orientation and no `UIRequiresFullScreen` (G-08), `MinimumOSVersion 26.5` (G-15), `UIStatusBarHidden true`, `LSApplicationCategoryType public.app-category.shopping`, `CFBundleURLSchemes ["patina"]`, and permission strings that come from `INFOPLIST_KEY_*` build settings which silently override differently-worded copies in the tracked `Patina/Info.plist` (G-07). The build transcript also shows `appintentsmetadataprocessor … warning: Metadata extraction skipped. No AppIntents.framework dependency found.` (G-14).
